But area might increase enough to reduce J (J=2n/M/S^2 with M=1). So key: find A with A^2 coefficients ≤1 and maximal total sum. This is exactly maximize S under all b≤1. Upper bound area ≤2n-1, giving J≥2n/(2n-1)≈1. Half-binomial area S^2 ~4n/π≈1.27n, compared to max area 2n. Need area ratio to tail ~1.9/2? Target J=1.5 ⇒ S^2=2n/1.5=1.333n (for M=1). So only 4.4% more area than half-binomial. Need make tail area increase by ~0.06n (since half area 1.273n). Feasible.

So problem reduces to: maximize area under B=A^2 with B≤1, A length n nonnegative. This is like find nonnegative polynomial A degree n-1 with coefficientwise square ≤1, maximize A(1). This is a well-defined extremal problem. Maybe can be solved via linear programming over A? The constraints are b_k≤1; objective linear A(1). Nonconvex due to products, but maybe maximizer has many b_k=1 (active) and can be found by alternation.

Algorithm for smooth max:
- θ = log a - logsumexp? Since normalize a after each step, we can store θ up to additive constant; set a = softmax(θ).
- Compute b, p=softmax(tau b) (or p=hard active normalized).
- h = 2 * cross(a,p) (gradient wrt a of M_τ).
- For softmax parameter, gradient wrt θ = a*(h - ∑ a h). To perform descent: θ ← θ - lr * grad_θ. Equivalently θ_i ← θ_i - lr*a_i*(h_i-mean). Then renormalize (softmax). This is not same as multiplicative a_i *= exp(-lr*(h_i-mean))? Since θ_i update subtract lr*a_i*(...); after normalization, a_i multiplied by exp(-lr*(h_i-mean)). Yes approximate. We can directly update a_i *= exp(-eta*(h_i-mean)) and normalize. This is entropy mirror descent. It preserves positivity. Learning rate eta. This is usually stable for nonnegative. For high tau, h spikes at pairs of peaks; reduces them.
- Can add momentum in θ: v = β v - η grad_θ; θ += v. But mirror momentum maybe.
- Line search: after update normalize, compute exact J; if worse, shrink eta. Use backtracking. Or use Polyak step based on J.

Active p selection function `make_p(b, gamma)`:
```
M=b.max()
if M<=0: return uniform
T = M/gamma
if T<=0: T=1e-300
logit=(b-M)/T
logit=np.clip(logit, -100, 0)
p=np.exp(logit)
s=p.sum()
if s<=0: p=np.full(len(b),1/len(b))
else p/=s
```
If b has exact plateaus, good. Gamma maybe 20 to 200. High gamma p hard; low gamma smoother.

Mirror step function:
```
def mirror_update(a, p, n, N, eta=0.5, mode='logratio', gamma_current? no)
  fa=... b... but g needs p. Maybe compute g in optimizer after b.
```
In optimizer loop:
- Have N fixed, fa, b, M, J.
- p=make_p(b,gamma)
- fp, g=... (fa*conj(fp))
- Optionally center g: mean_g = np.mean(g) or average with weights? For reciprocal use log.
- Candidate generation:

Pseudo:
```
def optimize_mirror(a, budget_s=30.0, max_iters=10000, gamma_start=5.0, gamma_end=200.0, lr_candidates=(0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0), sym=False):
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-15); a /= a.sum(); n=len(a)
    N = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    best_a=a; best_J=internal_J(a)
    gamma=gamma_start
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
      if time>budget: break
      # anneal gamma
      frac=min(1, it/max(1, iters_total?))
      gamma = gamma_start + (gamma_end-gamma_start)*min(1, it//50/?)
      fa=fft(a,N); b=irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2n-1]; M=b.max(); J=2nM
      if J<best_J: best...
      p=make_p(b,gamma)
      fp=rfft(p,N); g=irfft(fa*conj(fp),N)[:n]
      # if any g<=0 due to zeros, add floor relative
      floor = max(1e-15, np.mean(g)*1e-12)
      gsafe=np.maximum(g, floor)
      mg=np.exp(np.mean(np.log(gsafe)))
      d=np.log(gsafe/mg)
      # maybe only update if variance > tol
      var=np.var(d)
      if var < 1e-10: break
      improved=False
      for lr in lr_candidates:
         # direction: a *= exp(-lr*d)
         ratio=np.exp(-lr*d)
         # clip ratio e.g. [1e-6,1e6]
         ratio=np.clip(ratio,1e-6,1e6)
         an=a*ratio; an=np.maximum(an,0); an/=an.sum()
         Jn=internal_J(an) # maybe compute fast only? full
         if Jn < best_J*(1-1e-12) or Jn < J*(1-1e-10):
            a=an; improved=True; break
      if not improved:
          # try smaller lrs
          for lr in [0.03,0.01,0.003]: ...
          if still no: no_improve+=1; if no_improve>20: break
      else: no_improve=0
    return best_a,best_J
```

Initial half-binomial function:
```
def half_binomial(n):
  c=np.empty(n); c[0]=1
  for i in range(1,n): c[i]=c[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
  c=np.maximum(c,1e-30); c/=c.sum(); return c
```
This O(n). For n=50000 too slow but we won't.

    for it...
      if time>deadline: break
      fa... b... M=b.max(); J=2nM
      if J<best...
      g=lp_best_response(a,M)
      if g is None: break
      g=np.maximum(g,0); s=g.sum(); if s<1e-10: break
      g/=s
      base_t = 0.5 if it<... maybe decreasing
      for t in [base_t, base_t*0.5, base_t*0.25,0.02,0.005]:
         trial=np.maximum((1-t)*a + t*g,0); trial/=trial.sum(); Jt=...
         if Jt<best-J: a=trial; best=...
      if no improve: no_improve++; if >15 break
    return best
```

_LBFGS_N = 0
_LBFGS_NFFT = 0
_LBFGS_TAU = 100.0

def _lbfgs_obj(phi):
   a = np.exp(phi - np.max(phi)); a/=a.sum()
   N=_LBFGS_NFFT; n=_LBFGS_N
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N); b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]; M=b.max(); shifted=_LBFGS_TAU*(b-M); s=np.sum(np.exp(shifted)); return M + np.log(s)/_LBFGS_TAU
```

```
def _lbfgs_grad(phi):
   a...; p=make_p? Use softmax of tau b with stable tau. p from shifted.

   return a*(h - np.dot(a,h))
```
This is gradient of M_tau? Derivative of M_tau = b_max + (1/tau)log∑e^{tau(b-max)}. Since b_max term derivative = M_subgrad; the smooth expression derivative is (1/tau)∑e... *? Better use `M_tau = (1/tau) logsumexp(tau b)` directly. Compute `m = tau*b; m_shifted=m-m.max(); val = (m.max() + log(sum(exp(m_shifted))))/tau`. Gradient with p=softmax(tau b) = exp_shifted/sum. So use p from softmax tau b (stable). Good.
```

def generate_starts(n, rng, parent=None):
  starts=[]
  if parent is not None:
    p=resample(parent,n); starts.append((p,'parent'))
    p2=np.maximum(p + rng.normal(0,0.02,p.size),0); starts.append
    p3=np.maximum(p + rng.normal(0,0.05,p.size),0); starts.append
    p4=resample(parent[::-1],n); starts.append
  c=half_binomial(n); starts.append(c)
  # offset powers
  idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
  for beta in [0.1,0.5,1.0,3.0]:
     a=(idx+beta+0.5)**-0.5; starts.append(a)
  # exponential tilts of half binomial
  for th in [-0.8,-0.4,-0.2,0.2,0.5]:
     a=c* np.exp(-th*idx/(n-1)); starts.append
  # two endpoint small
  for mu in [0.05,0.15,0.3]:
     a=c + mu*c[::-1]; starts.append
  # random smooth
  t=idx/(n-1)
  for _ in range(3):
     a=1.0
     for f in rng.integers(1,5,size=4): a += 0.2*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*t + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
     a=np.maximum(a,0.05); starts.append
  # normalize and remove duplicates? Simple: normalize, positive, if sum>0.
  # Return list arrays.
```

Top-level globals for parametric:

_PARAM_N = 0
_PARAM_NFFT = 0
_PARAM_BASE_LOG = None
_PARAM_BASIS = None

def _parametric_objective(x):
    n = _PARAM_N
    loga = _PARAM_BASE_LOG.copy()
    for k in range(len(x)):
        if x[k] != 0.0:
            loga = loga + x[k] * _PARAM_BASIS[k]
    loga = np.clip(loga, -80.0, 80.0)
    a = np.exp(loga - np.max(loga))
    s = a.sum()
    if s <= 0: return 1e100
    a /= s
    J = internal_J_N(a, _PARAM_NFFT)
    return J if np.isfinite(J) else 1e100
```

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix

def next_power_of_two(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()
```

`fast_conv_norm(a, N=None)`: 
```
def fast_conv(a, N=None):
  a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64); n=len(a)
  if N is None: N=fft_len(n)
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
  b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]
  b[np.abs(b)<1e-15]=0.0
  return b
```
No clip negative? Could leave. For max, negative not issue. For p, use max(0,b). We can `b = np.maximum(b,0.0)` to ensure p. But small negative could affect g? b only for p; clip. `return np.maximum(b,0)` might alter sum slightly but okay.

`internal_J_N(a, N=None)`:
```
def internal_J_N(a, N=None):
  a=np.asarray(a,bool? no)
  a=np.maximum(a,0.0); s=a.sum()
  if a.size==0 or s<1e-8: return inf
  an=a/s
  if N is None: N=fft_len(a.size)
  fa=np.fft.rfft(an,N); b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*len(an)-1]
  M=float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
  if not np.isfinite(M) or M<0: M=0.0
  return float(2*an.size*M)
```
Since an sum1, J=2nM. Good.

`convolve2(x,y,N)`:
```
def convolve2(x,y,N=None):
   n=len(x); m=len(y)
   if N is None: N=next_power_of_two(n+m-1)
   fx=np.fft.rfft(x,N); fy=np.fft.rfft(y,N)
   return np.fft.irfft(fx*fy,N)[:n+m-1]
```
Used in LP.

`cross_g(a,p,N)`:
```
def cross_g(a,p,N=None):
  n=len(a); L=2*n-1
  if N is None: N=fft_len(n)
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
  pr=np.zeros(N); pr[:L]=p[::-1]
  fr=np.fft.rfft(pr)
  conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fr,N)
  idx=2*n-2 - np.arange(n)
  return np.asarray(np.real(conv[idx]), dtype=np.float64)
```
This allocates pr zeros each call. Could optimize by prealloc in optimizer: `pr_full = np.zeros(N); pr_full[:L]=p[::-1]; fr=rfft(pr_full)`. But function okay. In mirror, we already compute fa; we need fr. We can integrate to avoid double rfft of a? We'll compute directly in mirror:

b=...
p=...
pr=np.zeros(N); pr[:L]=p[::-1]
fr=rfft(pr)
conv=irfft(fa*fr,N)
g=conv[2*n-2 - arange(n)]
```

`make_p_from_b(b, gamma, mix=0.0)`:
```
def make_p_from_b(b, gamma=50.0, mix=0.0):
  L=len(b)
  if L==0: return None
  M=float(b.max())
  if M<=1e-300:
     return np.full(L,1.0/L)
  # peak softmax
  T=M/max(gamma,1e-12)
  if T<=0: T=1e-300
  logits=(b-M)/T
  logits=np.clip(logits, -100.0, 0.0)
  p=np.exp(logits)
  s=p.sum()
  if s<=1e-300:
     p=np.full(L,1.0/L)
  else: p/=s
  if mix>0:
     p_all=b/b.sum() if b.sum()>0 else np.full(L,1.0/L)
     p=(1-mix)*p + mix*p_all
  return p
```
If gamma large and only one peak, p one. For half-binomial many exact max, logits zero for max; p uniform over max. Good.

a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,float),0); s=a.sum(); if s<=0: return 1/len; return a/s
```

Screening function:
```
def screen_starts(starts, n, K=4, must_indices=None):
   N=fft_len(n); scored=[]
   for idx,a in enumerate(starts):
      an=normalize_positive(a)
      J=internal_J_N(an,N)
      if finite: scored.append((J,idx,an))
   scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
   # choose top K plus must
   chosen=[]
   if must_indices: for idx in must_indices if valid add
   for J,idx,an in scored: if len(chosen)>=K break; if idx not in chosen: chosen.append((J,an))
   # sort by J for optimization order
   chosen.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
   return chosen
```
But if K includes must and top duplicates, handle.

starts_tagged = [[a,'parent0'], ... [half,'half']]
```
Screen returns arrays in order.

`run_mirror` needs `rng` for perturbation. We'll pass.

Let's write `mirror_optimize` in full:
```
def run_mirror_optimize(a, deadline, rng=None, gamma_start=5.0, gamma_end=200.0, mix_start=0.2, mix_end=0.0, max_iters=100000, min_time=0.5):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng()
    n=a.size; N=fft_len(n); L=2*n-1
    best_a=a.copy(); best_J=internal_J_N(a,N); current_J=best_J
    no_improve=0; it=0
    # maybe use current b and fa from first? Loop
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iters:
        start_iter=time.monotonic()
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
        b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
        b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
        M=float(b.max())
        current_J=2*n*M
        if current_J < best_J:
            best_J=current_J; best_a=a.copy()
        # p
        frac = min(1.0, it / max(1, 200))
        gamma = gamma_start + (gamma_endgamma_start)*frac
        mix = mix_start + (mix_end-mix_start)*frac
        p=make_p_from_b(b,gamma,mix)
        pr=np.zeros(N); pr[:L]=p[::-1]
        fr=np.fft.rfft(pr)
        conv=np.fft.irfft(fa*fr,N)
        g=conv[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)].copy()
        # ensure g positive
        min_g = float(np.min(g)) if g.size else 0.0
        floor = max(1e-300, 1e-12 * max(1e-300, np.mean(g)))
        g = np.maximum(g, floor)
        logg=np.log(g)
        mg=np.exp(float(np.mean(logg)))
        d=logg - np.log(mg)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
            # fallback gradient? break
            break
        maxd=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if maxd < 1e-9:
            break
        # adaptive lrs
        scale = 1.0 / max(0.5, maxd)
        lrs=[scale, 0.5*scale, 0.25*scale, 0.125*scale]
        # avoid duplicates/too small
        improved=False
        for lr in lrs:
            if lr <= 0: continue
            # ratio = (mg/g)^lr = exp(-lr*d)
            ratio=np.exp(-lr*d)
            ratio=np.clip(ratio,1e-12,1e12)
            an=a*ratio
            s=an.sum()
            if s<=1e-8: continue
            an/=s
            Jn=internal_J_N(an,N)
            if np.isfinite(Jn) and Jn < best_J:
                a=an; current_J=Jn
                if Jn < best_J:
                   best_J=Jn; best_a=a.copy()
                improved=True; no_improve=0
                break
        if improved:
            it+=1
            continue
        # gradient fallback
        h=2.0*g
        meanh=float(np.dot(a,h))
        grad=h - meanh
        gnorm=float(np.max(np.abs(grad)))
        if gnorm > 1e-300:
            rel=grad/gnorm
            for step in (0.02,0.01,0.003,0.001):
                an=a - step*rel
                an=np.maximum(an,0.0)
                s=an.sum()
                if s<=1e-8: continue
                an/=s
                Jn=internal_J_N(an,N)
                if np.isfinite(Jn) and Jn < best_J:
                    a=an; best_J=Jn; best_a=a.copy(); improved=True; no_improve=0; break
        if improved:
            it+=1
            continue
        # no improve
        no_improve += 1
        it += 1
        if no_improve > 15:
            # perturb
            noise = max(0.005, 0.08 / no_improve)
            a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            a = normalize_positive(a)
            no_improve = 0
            if time.monotonic() - start_iter > 2.0:
                # if iterations too slow? continue
                pass
        # if no improvement and iteration time too high? break?
        if no_improve > 60:
            break
    # final sym candidates?
    # try reversed/sym quickly
    for cand in (a[::-1], 0.5*(a+a[::-1])):
       an=normalize_positive(cand)
       Jn=internal_J_N(an,N)
       if Jn < best_J: best_J=Jn; best_a=an.copy()
    return best_a, best_J
```
But in no_improve perturb, we reset no_improve to 0; could loop forever with random. Maybe keep count global; use `fail_count`. If no improvement after many perturb, break. Use `stall=0`; if no improve stall++; if stall>5 break. Perturb every 10 stalls. Simpler:
```
stall=0
...
if not improved:
  stall+=1
  if stall in (10,20,30,40): perturb
  if stall>50: break
else stall=0
```
Good.

Check `current_J` update: If best_J not improve, but current_J maybe same. Good.

Potential bug: `ratio=np.exp(-lr*d)` where d=log(g/mg). If g high, d positive, ratio<1 reduce; low g increase. Good.

`scale = 1/max(0.5,maxd)` means max exponent change ~1 (if maxd>1, scale<1, max |lr*d|~1; if maxd<1, scale=1, max change <1 but lr first 1). Reasonable.

Maybe first lr should be 0.5 scale not scale to avoid overshoot. Use lrs `[0.7*scale,0.35*scale,0.175*scale,0.0875*scale]`. Let's pick `[1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.05] * scale`. If too large fails, smaller.

Line search criterion `Jn < best_J` ensures global improvement. If current step improves but not best? best is same. Good. It might reject tiny improvements due floating; okay.

If no candidate improves but a very small one does, we might break too early. We include small lrs down to 0.05*scale; if scale ~1, 0.05. Gradient fallback small. If still none, stall.

We can also try `ratio = np.power(mg/g, lr)` same.

Now, `run_mirror_optimize` final sym/reverse may improve. Good.

LP function:

def run_lp_mirror_descent(a, deadline, max_iters=200):
   a=normalize; n; N; best...
   stall=0
   it=0
   while time<deadline and it<max_iters:
      fa=rfft(a,N); b=... M
      if J<best...
      g=lp_best_response(a,M, time_limit=5)
      if g is None: break
      g=pos normalize
      base_t=0.6 if it<20 else 0.2; maybe decrease
      for t in (base_t, base_t*0.5, base_t*0.25,0.05,0.02,0.01):
         trial=np.maximum((1-t)*a+t*g,0); trial/=trial.sum(); Jt=internal_J_N(trial,N); if Jt<best: accept
      if accept stall=0 else stall++
      if stall>15 break
      it++
   return best
```
`lp_best_response` needs time limit. Use `linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': min(8, max(1, remaining/10)))}`. But if global deadline soon, limit small.

Build A each iter: `build_A_sparse`. For n maybe 512. If LP fails many, break.

`lp_best_response` code:
```
def lp_best_response(a, M, time_limit=10.0):
  n=len(a)
  A=build_A_sparse(n,a)
  c=-np.ones(n)
  b_ub=np.full(2*n-1, float(M))
  bounds=[(0,None)]*n
  try:
    r=linprog(c,A_ub=A,b_ub=b_ub,bounds=bounds,method='highs',options={'time_limit':time_limit})
    if r.success and r.x is not None:
       x=np.maximum(r.x,0)
       if x.sum()>1e-12: return x
  except Exception:
     pass
  return None
```
`A` nonzeros n^2. For n=1024, 1M; memory okay but each iter allocate; time maybe. Limit n<=768? We'll call if n<=1024 and remaining >120. Could be slow but acceptable.

Parametric search top-level:
```
def run_parametric_search(a, deadline, D=5, rng=None):
   if time.monotonic() >= deadline: return a
   a=normalize; n=a.size; N=fft_len(n)
   base_log=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   basis=[t, t*t, (t-0.5)*np.ones(n), (t-0.5)**2, (t-0.5)**3]
   # choose D
   global _PARAM_N, _PARAM_NFFT, _PARAM_BASE_LOG, _PARAM_BASIS
   _PARAM_N=n; _PARAM_NFFT=N; _PARAM_BASE_LOG=base_log; _PARAM_BASIS=basis[:D]
   best_a=a.copy(); best_J=internal_J_N(a,N)
   # zero start
   x0=np.zeros(D)
   res=minimize(_parametric_objective, x0, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':200,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-9,'adaptive':True})
   if res.x is not None:
      a_trial=evaluate_param_x(res.x)
      J=... update
   # random starts few
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng()
   for r in range(3):
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 10: break
      x0=rng.normal(0,0.3,D)
      res=minimize(... maxiter=50)
      ...
   # scalar tilt already included; maybe also blend reversed
   return best_a
```

def _apply_param(x):
  loga = _PARAM_BASE_LOG + sum...
  ... return normalized a
```

But _PARAM_BASIS list length fixed D; if x length differs? We'll pass D. Ensure.

`minimize` with Nelder-Mead may call objective many times; no time stop. maxiter 200, for D=5 maybe ~200*? SciPy NM iterations not function evals exactly; could be 1000 evals. Fine. For safety, maxiter=100 for large n. We can set based on n: if n<=640 maxiter=250 else 100.

Could the parametric search diverge to very peaked a with high J? It minimizes J, so no. Initial zero base maybe good. It may find small x. Good.

Pseudo main:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(60, budget_s) - 30
    tracker=ScoreTracker()
    # load parent
    parent=None
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,float)
       if p.ndim==1 and p.size>0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
          parent=np.maximum(p,0)
          if parent.sum()>0:
             tracker.update(parent, 'parent')
    except Exception:
       pass
    if tracker.best is None:
       a=half_binomial(512); tracker.update(a,'half512')
    # Determine lengths
    lengths=[]
    pn=tracker.best.size
    cand=[256,384,512,640,768,1024,1536]
    if pn: cand.extend([pn, max(64,int(pn*1.5)), max(64,int(pn//2))])
    # unique sorted by priority
    priority = {512:0,768:1,640:2,384:3,1024:4,1536:5,256:6}
    unique...
    # sort by (priority if in else 10, n)
    # maybe limit to first 6
    # for each plan, generate starts, screen, param maybe for first init, mirror
    reserve=60
    for n in lengths[:6]:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve: break
       if n<128 or n>2048: continue
       # time total
       if n in (512,): total=450
       elif n in (640,768): total=180
       elif n==1024: total=150
       elif n==384: total=90
       elif n==1536: total=120
       else total=80
       # do not exceed remaining
       total = min(total, max(20, deadline - time.monotonic() - reserve))
       if total < 15: break
       parent_n = tracker.best
       starts = generate_starts_tagged(n, rng, tracker.best)
       # screen
       K = 4 if n<=640 else 3
       chosen = screen_starts(starts, n, K, must_tags=('parent','half'))
       if not chosen: continue
       # parametric search on best chosen or half for time min(60,total*0.2)
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - reserve and n <=1536:
           param_deadline=min(time.monotonic()+min(60,total*0.25), deadline-reserve)
           # choose a base: maybe half_binomial(n) if exists else chosen[0][1]
           base = None
           for a,tag in starts: if tag=='half': base=a
           if base is None: base=chosen[0][1]
           pa=run_parametric_search(base, param_deadline, D=5, rng=rng)
           tracker.update(pa,'param')
       # now re-screen? Maybe chosen plus param; add param to pending if not in chosen? We can just run mirror on chosen; param result improved tracker; before mirror, if tracker.best length n, add to pending.
       pending=[]
       for J,a in chosen: pending.append(a)
       if tracker.best is not None and tracker.best.size==n:
           # add best if not close? just add
           pending.append(tracker.best)
       # also resample global best to n
       if tracker.best is not None and tracker.best.size != n:
           pending.append(resample_seq(tracker.best, n))
       # unique pending by hash? not needed
       per = total / len(pending)
       rem = len(pending)
       for idx,a in enumerate(pending):
           if time.monotonic() > deadline - reserve: break
           per = min(per, max(5.0, (deadline - reserve - time.monotonic())/max(1, rem)))
           dlt = time.monotonic()+per
           if dlt < time.monotonic()+5: continue
           # choose mirror params: initial broad
           best, J = run_mirror_optimize(a, deadline=min(dlt, deadline-reserve), rng=rng, gamma_start=5, gamma_end=150, mix_start=0.2, mix_end=0.05)
           tracker.update(best, f'mirror n={n}')
           rem -=1
    # Polish
    if tracker.best is not None:
       nbest=tracker.best.size
       # mirror polish best
       if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
          dlt=min(time.monotonic()+180, deadline-20)
          best,J=run_mirror_optimize(tracker.best, dlt, rng=rng, gamma_start=20, gamma_end=400, mix_start=0.05, mix_end=0.0)
          tracker.update(best,'polish')
       # sym/reverse starts
       for label, base in (('rev', tracker.best[::-1]), ('sym', 0.5*(tracker.best+tracker.best[::-1]))):
           if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20:
              dlt=min(time.monotonic()+60, deadline-20)
              best,J=run_mirror_optimize(normalize_positive(base), dlt, rng=rng, gamma_start=10, gamma_end=300, mix_start=0.05, mix_end=0.0)
              tracker.update(best,label)
       # length extension
       if tracker.best is not None and tracker.best.size < 2048 and time.monotonic() < deadline-20:
          new_n = min(2048, int(tracker.best.size*1.5) if tracker.best.size<1024 else tracker.best.size*2?)
          # choose 2x if <=2048 else 1536?
          ext_n = tracker.best.size*2 if tracker.best.size*2 <=2048 else max(tracker.best.size+256, 1536?) But if size>1024, 2x>2048, skip or ext_n=2048.
          if ext_n >= tracker.best.size+64:
             a_ext=resample_seq(tracker.best, ext_n)
             dlt=min(time.monotonic()+80, deadline-20)
             best,J=run_mirror_optimize(a_ext,dlt,rng=rng,gamma_start=5,gamma_end=200,mix_start=0.1,mix_end=0.0)
             tracker.update(best,'ext')
       # LP
       if tracker.best is not None and tracker.best.size <=1024 and time.monotonic() < deadline-30:
          dlt=min(time.monotonic()+150, deadline-30)
          best,J=run_lp_mirror_descent(tracker.best, dlt, max_iters=100)
          tracker.update(best,'lp')
       # L-BFGS
       if tracker.best is not None and tracker.best.size <=1536 and time.monotonic() < deadline-10:
          dlt=min(time.monotonic()+90, deadline-10)
          best=run_lbfgs_optimize(tracker.best, dlt, tau=100)
          tracker.update(best,'lbfgs')
          if time.monotonic() < deadline-10:
             best=run_lbfgs_optimize(tracker.best, time.monotonic()+40, tau=300)
             tracker.update(best,'lbfgs2')
    # final cleanup
    final = cleanup_sequence(tracker.best if tracker.best is not None else half_binomial(512))
    tracker.update(final,'final')
    # optional evaluate_sequence
    seq=[float(x) for x in tracker.best]
    try:
       score=float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
       if np.isfinite(score) and score < tracker.best_J:
          # don't change seq? score lower maybe due clipping? seq same.
          tracker.best_J=score
          print...
    except Exception:
       pass
    print final
    return [float(x) for x in tracker.best]
```
Need ensure `height_sequence_1` global may not exist; use `try: p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)` except NameError. In Python, undefined name raises; catch.

`run_lbfgs_optimize` function:
```
def run_lbfgs_optimize(a, deadline, tau=100.0):
   a=normalize; n; N
   phi=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   phi -= phi.mean() # center
   global _LBFGS_N, _LBFGS_NFFT, _LBFGS_TAU
   _LBFGS_N=n; _LBFGS_NFFT=N; _LBFGS_TAU=float(tau)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_J=internal_J_N(a,N)
   try:
     res=minimize(_lbfgs_objective, phi, jac=_lbfgs_gradient, method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':150,'maxfun':8000,'gtol':1e-10})
     if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
       bx=from_phi(res.x)
       J=internal_J_N(bx,N); if J<best_J: best=...
   except Exception as e: print
   return best_a
```
Need `_lbfgs_objective` and `_lbfgs_gradient` top-level. `from_phi` helper maybe top-level.

`_lbfgs_gradient` uses `_LBFGS_TAU` directly not gamma. Compute p:
```
m = tau*(b-M)
m=np.clip(m,-100,0); p=exp(m); p/=p.sum()
# cross_g using reverse p
pr=zeros; pr[:L]=p[::-1]; ...; g=...
h=2*g
return a*(h - np.dot(a,h))
```
If tau very large, m for non-peaks underflows to 0; okay.

`_lbfgs_objective` can use same p? Only value; value = (m.max + log(sum exp(m_shifted)))/tau. Need b. It will recompute b. Fine.

`_apply_phi` top-level:

def _phi_to_a(phi):
    z=phi - np.max(phi)
    a=np.exp(z); s=a.sum(); return a/s
```

Now, final cleanup:
```
def cleanup_sequence(a):
   a=normalize_positive(a)
   candidates=[a]
   a2=a.copy(); a2[a2<1e-12]=0
   if a2.sum()>0: candidates.append(normalize_positive(a2))
   candidates.append(a[::-1])
   cand_sym=0.5*(a+a[::-1]); if sum>0 candidates.append(normalize_positive(cand_sym))
   best=min(candidates,key=internal_J)
   return best
```
`internal_J` without N computes FFT length each; few calls okay.

Print output: tracker.update prints improvements. At final print `FINAL INTERNAL J`, and evaluate score.

Let's examine potential bugs:
- `np.maximum(a,0)` in tracker before normalize; if a has all zeros, skip.
- `resample_seq` for parent to n: if src size 1? handle.
- `generate_starts_tagged`: If parent is None, don't include parent tags. Must tags maybe not present; screen handles missing.
- In main length loop, `tracker.best` changes during loop; parent_n for generate uses current best. Good.
- Time limits: mirror may run exactly to deadline; internal trial may exceed tiny? It checks loop condition at top only; one iteration could exceed by FFT time small. Final deadline has reserve 30, okay.
- `evaluate_sequence` may not be defined in some environment? Problem says assume imported. In final try except NameError.
- Return type list floats. If tracker.best None, use half_binomial(128).

Now, can we reduce over-engineering risk? The code is long but should work. Need ensure no syntax errors, no nested closures. We'll write final carefully.

Let's test mentally small pieces.

`next_power_of_two`:
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```
For m=1, (0).bit_length=0 =>1. For m=2,1.bit_length=1=>2. Good.

`fft_len(n)`: for n=512, 3n-2=1534, next pow2 2048? Wait 1534 -> next 2048, not 1536. I earlier thought 1536. 3*512-2=1534, next 2048. That's a bit larger. Could use N=2048. Still okay. If we only need cross with reversed p length L=1023, conv length n+L-1 =1534, next 2048. Good. For n=1024, 3070 ->4096.

This increases cost but safe. Could choose N=next_pow2(2n-1) for b and a smaller N for g using correlation sign? Maybe to speed. But safe is fine. For n=2048, N=8192 (6142 ->8192). Good.

`internal_J_N` with N=2048 for n=512; fine.

`make_p_from_b`: If b max M, T=M/gamma. If gamma=5, T=0.2M, logits for b=0 -> -gamma=-5, p exp(-5)=0.0067; not too focused. gamma=200, T=0.005M, logits -200 for zero, exp underflow clipped -100 => p~3.7e-44, okay. We clip logits -100 not -200, so minimum relative p ~e-100. Good.

`mirror_optimize`: p mix with p_all. `p_all=b/b.sum()`. b.sum() should be ≈1 (normalized a), due FFT numeric; okay. If sum zero, uniform.

`ratio=np.exp(-lr*d)`, d clipped? We clip ratio but not d. d could be large if g floor. We can clip d before exp: `d=np.clip(d,-20,20)` to avoid overflow. Add. Also floor relative maybe prevents. Use `d = np.clip(logg - np.log(mg), -20.0, 20.0)`.

`mg=np.exp(np.mean(logg))` could overflow if logg large? logg ~ log a ~ few; okay.

`g = conv[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]`: For n=1, indices [0] works? 2*1-2=0, arange(1)=[0]. Good. But cross for n=1: a length1, p length1, pr length1, conv [a0p0], g[a0p0]. Good.

In mirror, if `np.any(~np.isfinite(g))`, break. Add check.

`internal_J_N` for an may have sum not 1 if an from trial normalized; it normalizes again, okay. It uses N maybe too small? We pass same N. For an length n, N valid.

LP `build_A_sparse` with `np.tile(a, n)` creates length n*n values a_j repeated for each i? Let's verify: We need for each row i (col i) and j, val a_j. `np.tile(a, n)` = [a0..a_{n-1}] repeated n times. `cols=np.repeat(i,n)` = [0 repeated n,1 repeated n,...]. `rows=(i[:,None]+j[None,:]).ravel()` = for i=0: 0..n-1; i=1:1..n; ... For row i first j=0 val a0; but A row k=i+j col i val a_j. For i=1,j=0 row1 col1 val a0. Correct. For each col i, vals are a_j for j=0..n-1. Tile correct. Memory: rows int64 8MB for n=1024 (1M*8), cols 8, vals 8, total 24MB plus sparse. Fine.

`linprog` with A sparse float. The matrix is nonnegative. `b_ub` all M. If M very small, constraints tight. Objective max sum g. If g vector from previous maybe warm start not used. Could set `options={'warm_start': True}`? HiGHS in scipy maybe supports? Not reliably. Ignore.

`lp_best_response` time limit: If global remaining small, limit smaller. We'll pass `time_limit=min(10, max(1, (deadline - time.monotonic()) / max(1, max_iters - it)))`? Simpler: 5s. If LP too slow, it returns fail. Use 8.

In LP line search, `base_t`: We can start 0.35 and reduce. For mirror descent, t=1 maybe too aggressive. Use `[0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]`. If best improves accept. Good.

L-BFGS: `_lbfgs_gradient` returns gradient of smooth objective wrt phi. Need ensure gradient sum zero? Since softmax, gradient has dot with 1 = sum a*(h - a h)=0. Good.

`_lbfgs_objective` value: use stable logsumexp. 
```
m = tau*b
mmax = m.max()
val = (mmax + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(m-mmax))))/tau
```

`generate` details:
```
def generate_starts_tagged(n, rng, parent=None):
    items=[]
    if parent is not None:
       p=resample_seq(parent,n); items.append((p,'parent'))
       p2=resample_seq(parent[::-1],n); items.append((p2,'parent_rev'))
       p3=np.maximum(p + rng.normal(0,0.02,n),1e-8); items.append((p3,'parent_noise'))
    # exact half binomial
    c=half_binomial(n); items.append((c,'half'))
    idx=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    denom=max(1,n-1)
    for beta in (0.1,0.5,1.0,3.0):
       a=(idx+beta+0.5)**-0.5; items.append((a,f'pow_{beta}'))
    for th in (-0.8,-0.4,-0.1,0.3):
       a=c*np.exp(-th*idx/denom); items.append((a,f'tilt_{th}'))
    for mu in (0.1,0.25,0.5):
       a=c+mu*c[::-1]; items.append((a,f'two_{mu}'))
    # random smooth 3
    t=idx/denom
    for r in range(3):
       a=1.0*ones
       for f in (1,2,3,5): amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3); a+=amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*t + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       a=np.maximum(a,0.05); items.append((a,f'rand_{r}'))
    # normalize/floor
    out=[]
    for a,tag in items:
       a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); s=a.sum(); if s>1e-8: out.append((a/s,tag))
    return out
```
Screening must tags: ('parent','half'). If parent_rev and parent are similar, okay.

```
_PARAM_N = 0
_PARAM_NSELF = 0
_PARAM_BASE_LOG = None
_PARAM_BASIS = []

def _param_apply(x):
   loga = _PARAM_BASE_LOG.copy()
   for k in range(len(x)):
       if x[k] != 0.0:
           loga = loga + float(x[k]) * _PARAM_BASIS[k]
   loga = np.clip(loga, -80.0, 80.0)
   z = loga - np.max(loga)
   a = np.exp(z)
   s = a.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(_PARAM_N)/_PARAM_N
   return a/s

def _param_objective(x):
   if x is None: return 1e100
   a = _param_apply(np.asarray(x, dtype=float))
   return internal_J_N(a, _PARAM_NSELF)
```

`run_parametric_search`:
```
def run_parametric_search(a, deadline, D=5, rng=None):
   if time.monotonic() >= deadline: return a
   a=normalize_positive(a)
   n=a.size
   global ...
   _PARAM_N=n; _PARAM_NSELF=fft_len_self(n); _PARAM_BASE_LOG=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300));
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   basis_full=[t, t*t, (t-0.5), (t-0.5)**2, (t-0.5)**3, t**4]
   D=min(D,len(basis_full)); _PARAM_BASIS=[np.asarray(b) for b in basis_full[:D]]
   best_a=a.copy(); best_J=internal_J_N(a,_PARAM_NSELF)
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng()
   # zero
   maxiter = 250 if n<=768 else 100
   try:
     res=minimize(_param_objective, np.zeros(D), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':maxiter,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-9,'adaptive':True})
     if res.x is not None:
       al=_param_apply(res.x); J=internal_J_N(al,_PARAM_NSELF); update
   except: pass
   # random starts 3 with small maxiter
   for r in range(3):
      if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 5: break
      x0=rng.normal(0,0.4,D)
      try:
         res=minimize(_param_objective, x0, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':80,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-8,'adaptive':True})
         ...
      except: pass
   return best_a
```

Now, L-BFGS: We need `_lbfgs_objective` and gradient maybe recompute b. In gradient, we compute b with Nself; p; g with Ncross. Use global `_LBFGS_NCROSS`. Code:
```
def _lbfgs_gradient(phi):
   a=_phi_to_a(phi)
   n=_LBFGS_N
   Nself=_LBFGS_NSELF; Ncross=_LBFGS_NCROSS; L=2*n-1
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,Nself)
   b=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,Nself)[:L]
   b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
   M=float(b.max())
   m=_LBFGS_TAU*(b-M)
   m=np.clip(m,-100.0,0.0)
   p=np.exp(m); s=p.sum()
   if s<=0: p=np.full(L,1.0/L)
   else: p/=s
   pr=np.zeros(Ncross); pr[:L]=p[::-1]
   fa_cross=np.fft.rfft(a,Ncross)
   fr=np.fft.rfft(pr)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(fa_cross*fr,Ncross)
   g=conv[2*n-2 - np.arange(n)]
   h=2.0*g
   return a*(h - float(np.dot(a,h)))
```

```python
"""
Hybrid non-linear search for minimizing
J(a) = 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a))^2  with a_i >= 0.

Main ingredients:
- Strong analytic initializers, especially the truncated (1-z)^(-1/2)
  binomial profile, which already gives J ~= pi/2 for large n.
- Log-space multiplicative mirror descent with a smoothly regularized
  active peak distribution.  This is the workhorse local refiner.
- Small parameterized global searches around good profiles.
- LP Frank-Wolfe style best-response polishing.
- L-BFGS polishing with a log-sum-exp approximation to max(conv(a,a)).
"""

import time
import numpy as np

from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog
from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def fft_len_self(n):
    return next_power_of_two(max(2 * int(n) - 1, 1))


def fft_len_cross(n):
    return next_power_of_two(max(3 * int(n) - 2, 1))


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(128, dtype=np.float64) / 128.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / a.size
    return a / s


def internal_J_N(a, N=None):
    """
    Fast evaluation of J for nonnegative a.  If N is given it must be
    large enough for self-convolution of len(a).
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    if N is None:
        N = fft_len_self(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * M)


def internal_J(a):
    return internal_J_N(a, None)


def resample_seq(src, dest_n):
    src = np.asarray(src, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    dest_n = int(dest_n)
    if dest_n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    if src.size == 0:
        return np.ones(dest_n, dtype=np.float64) / dest_n
    if src.size == 1:
        return np.full(dest_n, float(src[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    if src.size == dest_n:
        return np.maximum(src, 0.0)
    t_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, src.size, dtype=np.float64)
    t_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, dest_n, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.maximum(np.interp(t_new, t_old, src), 0.0)


def add_unique(lst, a):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    for old in lst:
        if old.size == a.size:
            d = float(np.dot(old, a))
            if np.isfinite(d) and d > 0.999999:
                return
    lst.append(a)


def half_binomial(n):
    """Coefficients of (1-z)^(-1/2), truncated to length n, normalized."""
    n = int(n)
    c = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        c[i] = c[i - 1] * ((2.0 * i - 1.0) / (2.0 * i))
    c = np.maximum(c, 1e-300)
    c /= c.sum()
    return c


def make_p_from_b(b, gamma=50.0, mix=0.0):
    """
    A probability distribution over convolution lags, concentrated on peaks
    of b but with a mild broad component when mix > 0.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    L = b.size
    if L == 0:
        return np.ones(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = float(np.max(b))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)

    T = M / max(float(gamma), 1e-12)
    if T <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(T):
        T = 1e-300
    logits = (b - M) / T
    logits = np.clip(logits, -100.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(logits)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s

    if mix > 0.0:
        bs = float(b.sum())
        if np.isfinite(bs) and bs > 1e-300:
            p_all = b / bs
            p = (1.0 - float(mix)) * p + float(mix) * p_all
        else:
            p = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    return p


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Initializers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def generate_starts_tagged(n, rng, parent=None):
    n = int(n)
    items = []
    if parent is not None:
        p = resample_seq(parent, n)
        p = normalize_positive(p)
        items.append((p, "parent"))

        pr = resample_seq(parent[::-1], n)
        pr = normalize_positive(pr)
        items.append((pr, "parent_rev"))

        pn = np.maximum(p + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, n), 1e-10)
        pn = normalize_positive(pn)
        items.append((pn, "parent_noise"))

    c = half_binomial(n)
    items.append((c, "half"))

    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    denom = max(1, n - 1)

    # Powered endpoint singular profiles.
    for beta in (0.1, 0.5, 1.0, 3.0):
        a = (idx + beta + 0.5) ** -0.5
        items.append((a, "pow_%s" % beta))

    # Exponential tilts of the binomial profile.
    for th in (-0.8, -0.4, -0.1, 0.3):
        a = c * np.exp(-th * idx / denom)
        items.append((a, "tilt_%s" % th))

    # Mild two-endpoint mixtures.
    for mu in (0.1, 0.25, 0.5):
        a = c + float(mu) * c[::-1]
        items.append((a, "two_%s" % mu))

    # Smooth random low-frequency profiles.
    t = idx / denom
    for r in range(3):
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for f in (1, 2, 3, 5):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.30)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            a = a + float(amp) * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * t + ph)
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.05)
        items.append((a, "rand_%d" % r))

    out = []
    for a, tag in items:
        a = np.maximum(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), 1e-12)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if s > 1e-12 and np.isfinite(s):
            out.append((a / s, tag))
    return out


def screen_starts(items, n, K=4, must_tags=()):
    n = int(n)
    N = fft_len_self(n)
    scored = []
    score_map = {}

    for idx, (a, tag) in enumerate(items):
        J = internal_J_N(a, N)
        if np.isfinite(J):
            score_map[idx] = float(J)
            scored.append((float(J), idx, a, tag))

    scored.sort()

    chosen = []
    for tag in must_tags:
        for idx, (_, t) in enumerate(items):
            if t == tag and idx in score_map and idx not in chosen:
                chosen.append(idx)
                break

    for J, idx, a, tag in scored:
        if len(chosen) >= K:
            break
        if idx not in chosen:
            chosen.append(idx)

    result = []
    for idx in chosen:
        if idx in score_map:
            result.append((score_map[idx], idx, items[idx][0], items[idx][1]))
    result.sort(key=lambda unused: 0)  # no-op placeholder removed below?
    # Python lambda is disallowed; replace with tuple sort.
    result = sorted(result, key=None) if False else result
    result.sort()
    return [(a, tag) for J, idx, a, tag in result]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Parameterized search (small-dimensional global profile tuning)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

_PARAM_N = 0
_PARAM_NSELF = 0
_PARAM_BASE_LOG = None
_PARAM_BASIS = []


def _param_apply(x):
    if _PARAM_BASE_LOG is None or _PARAM_N <= 0:
        return None
    loga = _PARAM_BASE_LOG.copy()
    for k in range(len(x)):
        if k < len(_PARAM_BASIS):
            v = float(x[k])
            if v != 0.0:
                loga = loga + v * _PARAM_BASIS[k]
    loga = np.clip(loga, -80.0, 80.0)
    z = loga - np.max(loga)
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(_PARAM_N, dtype=np.float64) / _PARAM_N
    return a / s


def _param_objective(x):
    a = _param_apply(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64))
    if a is None:
        return 1e100
    J = internal_J_N(a, _PARAM_NSELF)
    return J if np.isfinite(J) else 1e100


def run_parametric_search(a, deadline, D=5, rng=None):
    global _PARAM_N, _PARAM_NSELF, _PARAM_BASE_LOG, _PARAM_BASIS

    t_now = time.monotonic()
    if t_now >= deadline:
        return normalize_positive(a)

    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)

    _PARAM_N = int(n)
    _PARAM_NSELF = fft_len_self(n)
    _PARAM_BASE_LOG = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    centered = t - 0.5
    basis_full = [
        t,
        t * t,
        centered,
        centered * centered,
        centered ** 3,
        t ** 4,
    ]
    D = max(1, min(int(D), len(basis_full)))
    _PARAM_BASIS = [np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64) for b in basis_full[:D]]

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_J = internal_J_N(best_a, _PARAM_NSELF)

    maxiter0 = 250 if n <= 768 else 100
    maxiter_r = 80 if n <= 768 else 50

    starts = [np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)]
    for r in range(3):
        starts.append(rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, D, dtype=np.float64))

    for si, x0 in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 2.0:
            break
        maxit = maxiter0 if si == 0 else maxiter_r
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _param_objective,
                x0,
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={
                    "maxiter": int(maxit),
                    "xatol": 1e-5,
                    "fatol": 1e-9,
                },
            )
            if res is not None and res.x is not None:
                at = _param_apply(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
                if at is not None:
                    Jt = internal_J_N(at, _PARAM_NSELF)
                    if np.isfinite(Jt) and Jt < best_J:
                        best_J = Jt
                        best_a = at.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    return best_a


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Mirror descent in log-space
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def run_mirror_optimize(
    a,
    deadline,
    rng=None,
    gamma_start=5.0,
    gamma_end=200.0,
    mix_start=0.20,
    mix_end=0.05,
    max_iters=1000000,
):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)

    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    Nself = fft_len_self(n)
    Ncross = fft_len_cross(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    idx_cross = (2 * n - 2) - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_J = internal_J_N(a, Nself)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iters:
        it += 1

        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Nself)
        b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Nself)[:L]
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        cur_J = float(2.0 * n * M)

        if np.isfinite(cur_J) and cur_J < best_J:
            best_J = cur_J
            best_a = a.copy()

        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        frac = min(1.0, it / max(1, 250))
        gamma = float(gamma_start) + (float(gamma_end) - float(gamma_start)) * frac
        mix = float(mix_start) + (float(mix_end) - float(mix_start)) * frac

        p = make_p_from_b(b, gamma=gamma, mix=mix)

        pr = np.zeros(Ncross, dtype=np.float64)
        pr[:L] = p[::-1]
        fr = np.fft.rfft(pr)
        fa_cross = np.fft.rfft(a, Ncross)
        conv = np.fft.irfft(fa_cross * fr, Ncross)
        g = np.asarray(conv[idx_cross], dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.real(g)

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        mean_g = float(np.mean(g))
        floor = max(1e-300, 1e-12 * max(1e-300, abs(mean_g)))
        g = np.maximum(g, floor)

        logg = np.log(g)
        log_mg = float(np.mean(logg))
        d = logg - log_mg
        d = np.clip(d, -20.0, 20.0)
        maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd < 1e-9:
            break

        scale = 1.0 / max(0.5, maxd)
        candidate_lrs = (1.0 * scale, 0.5 * scale, 0.25 * scale, 0.125 * scale, 0.0625 * scale)

        improved = False
        for lr in candidate_lrs:
            if lr <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(lr):
                continue
            ratio = np.exp(-lr * d)
            ratio = np.clip(ratio, 1e-14, 1e14)
            an = a * ratio
            s = float(an.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
                continue
            an /= s
            Jn = internal_J_N(an, Nself)
            if np.isfinite(Jn) and Jn < best_J:
                a = an
                best_J = Jn
                best_a = an.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            continue

        # Fallback: small Euclidean projected gradient step using the same
        # smoothed active distribution.
        h = 2.0 * g
        mean_h = float(np.dot(a, h))
        grad = h - mean_h
        gmax = float(np.max(np.abs(grad))) if grad.size else 0.0
        if np.isfinite(gmax) and gmax > 1e-300:
            relgrad = grad / gmax
            for step in (0.02, 0.008, 0.003, 0.001):
                an = np.maximum(a - step * relgrad, 0.0)
                s = float(an.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
                    continue
                an /= s
                Jn = internal_J_N(an, Nself)
                if np.isfinite(Jn) and Jn < best_J:
                    a = an
                    best_J = Jn
                    best_a = an.copy()
                    improved = True
                    break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            continue

        stall += 1
        if stall in (8, 16, 24, 32, 40):
            noise = max(0.004, 0.06 / float(stall))
            a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            a = normalize_positive(a)

        if stall > 50:
            break

    # Cheap symmetry checks on the final local solution.
    for cand in (a[::-1], 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])):
        cand = normalize_positive(cand)
        Jc = internal_J_N(cand, Nself)
        if np.isfinite(Jc) and Jc < best_J:
            best_J = Jc
            best_a = cand.copy()

    return best_a, best_J


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# LP best-response polish
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def build_A_sparse(n, a):
    n = int(n)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n <= 0:
        return csr_matrix((0, 0), dtype=np.float64)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    rows = (i[:, None] + j[None, :]).ravel()
    cols = np.repeat(i, n)
    vals = np.tile(a, n)
    return csr_matrix((vals, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n), dtype=np.float64)


def lp_best_response(a, M, time_limit=8.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    M = float(M)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    A = build_A_sparse(n, a)
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.full(2 * n - 1, M, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    try:
        res = linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"time_limit": float(time_limit)},
        )
        if res.success is True and res.x is not None:
            x = np.maximum(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64), 0.0)
            if x.size == n and np.isfinite(x.sum()) and x.sum() > 1e-14:
                return x
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def run_lp_mirror_descent(a, deadline, max_iters=120):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0 or n > 1200:
        return a

    Nself = fft_len_self(n)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_J = internal_J_N(a, Nself)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < max_iters:
        it += 1
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Nself)
        b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Nself)[:2 * n - 1]
        b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
        M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
        cur_J = float(2.0 * n * M)
        if np.isfinite(cur_J) and cur_J < best_J:
            best_J = cur_J
            best_a = a.copy()

        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        rem = max(0.5, deadline - time.monotonic())
        tl = min(8.0, max(0.5, rem / max(1, max_iters - it + 1)) * 2.0)
        g = lp_best_response(a, M, time_limit=tl)
        if g is None:
            if stall >= 3:
                break
            stall += 1
            continue

        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(gs) or gs <= 1e-14:
            stall += 1
            continue
        g = g / gs

        improved = False
        for t in (0.35, 0.20, 0.10, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01):
            trial = np.maximum((1.0 - float(t)) * a + float(t) * g, 0.0)
            s = float(trial.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
                continue
            trial = trial / s
            Jt = internal_J_N(trial, Nself)
            if np.isfinite(Jt) and Jt < best_J:
                a = trial
                best_J = Jt
                best_a = a.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 14:
                break

    return best_a


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# L-BFGS polish with smooth max
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

_LBFGS_N = 0
_LBFGS_NSELF = 0
_LBFGS_NCROSS = 0
_LBFGS_TAU = 100.0
_LBFGS_IDX = None


def _phi_to_a(phi):
    z = np.asarray(phi, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    a = np.exp(z)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / z.size
    return a / s


def _lbfgs_objective(phi):
    if _LBFGS_N <= 0:
        return 1e100
    a = _phi_to_a(phi)
    n = _LBFGS_N
    N = _LBFGS_NSELF
    L = 2 * n - 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    tau = float(_LBFGS_TAU)
    m = tau * b
    mm = float(np.max(m)) if m.size else 0.0
    shifted = np.clip(m - mm, -100.0, 100.0)
    val = (mm + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shifted))))) / tau if tau > 0 else mm
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else 1e100


def _lbfgs_gradient(phi):
    if _LBFGS_N <= 0:
        return None
    a = _phi_to_a(phi)
    n = _LBFGS_N
    Nself = _LBFGS_NSELF
    Ncross = _LBFGS_NCROSS
    L = 2 * n - 1

    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, Nself)
    b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, Nself)[:L]
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    M = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    tau = float(_LBFGS_TAU)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0 or tau <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    m = tau * (b - M)
    m = np.clip(m, -100.0, 0.0)
    p = np.exp(m)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s

    pr = np.zeros(Ncross, dtype=np.float64)
    pr[:L] = p[::-1]
    fr = np.fft.rfft(pr)
    fa_cross = np.fft.rfft(a, Ncross)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fa_cross * fr, Ncross)

    idx = (2 * n - 2) - np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    g = np.asarray(conv[idx], dtype=np.float64)
    h = 2.0 * g
    return a * (h - float(np.dot(a, h)))


def run_lbfgs_optimize(a, deadline, tau=100.0):
    global _LBFGS_N, _LBFGS_NSELF, _LBFGS_NCROSS, _LBFGS_TAU

    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0 or time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        return a

    _LBFGS_N = n
    _LBFGS_NSELF = fft_len_self(n)
    _LBFGS_NCROSS = fft_len_cross(n)
    _LBFGS_TAU = float(tau)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_J = internal_J_N(best_a, _LBFGS_NSELF)

    phi = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    phi = phi - float(np.mean(phi))

    maxfun = 3500 if n <= 768 else 1800
    maxiter = 160 if n <= 768 else 90
    try:
        res = minimize(
            _lbfgs_objective,
            phi,
            jac=_lbfgs_gradient,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            options={
                "maxiter": int(maxiter),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "gtol": 1e-10,
            },
        )
        if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            at = _phi_to_a(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
            Jt = internal_J_N(at, _LBFGS_NSELF)
            if np.isfinite(Jt) and Jt < best_J:
                best_a = at.copy()
                best_J = Jt
    except Exception:
        pass

    return best_a


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cleanup / final candidate polishing
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def cleanup_sequence(a):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    if a.size == 0:
        return half_binomial(128)

    candidates = [a]

    a0 = a.copy()
    a0[a0 < 1e-12] = 0.0
    if a0.sum() > 1e-12:
        candidates.append(normalize_positive(a0))

    candidates.append(a[::-1])
    sym = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    if sym.sum() > 1e-12:
        candidates.append(normalize_positive(sym))

    best = a
    best_J = internal_J(a)
    for c in candidates:
        J = internal_J(c)
        if np.isfinite(J) and J < best_J:
            best_J = J
            best = c
    return best


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Score tracker
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

class ScoreTracker:
    def __init__(self):
        self.best = None
        self.best_J = float("inf")
        self.t0 = time.monotonic()

    def update(self, a, label=""):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
            return
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
            return
        an = a / s
        J = internal_J_N(an, None)
        if np.isfinite(J) and J < self.best_J:
            old = self.best_J
            self.best = an.copy()
            self.best_J = J
            rel = 0.0 if not np.isfinite(old) else (old - J) / max(1e-300, old)
            if not np.isfinite(old) or rel > 1e-7:
                elapsed = time.monotonic() - self.t0
                print(
                    "  updated J=%.9f n=%d rel=%.3e %s t=%.1fs"
                    % (self.best_J, self.best.size, rel, label, elapsed),
                    flush=True,
                )

    def report(self, label=""):
        if self.best is None:
            print("  %s no sequence found" % label, flush=True)
        else:
            print(
                "  %s best J=%.9f n=%d t=%.1fs"
                % (label, self.best_J, self.best.size, time.monotonic() - self.t0),
                flush=True,
            )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    total = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start_t + max(20.0, total - 25.0)

    tracker = ScoreTracker()

    # Load previous construction if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            parent = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if parent.sum() > 0.0:
                tracker.update(parent, "parent")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if tracker.best is None:
        fallback = half_binomial(512)
        tracker.update(fallback, "fallback_half")
    else:
        print("Loaded parent: n=%d J=%.9f" % (tracker.best.size, tracker.best_J), flush=True)

    # Choose lengths to explore.
    base_n = int(tracker.best.size)
    cand_lengths = [256, 384, 512, 640, 768, 1024, 1536]
    if base_n > 0:
        cand_lengths.extend([base_n, max(64, int(base_n * 1.5)), max(64, int(base_n // 2))])

    unique_lengths = []
    for x in cand_lengths:
        x = int(x)
        if 128 <= x <= 2048 and x not in unique_lengths:
            unique_lengths.append(x)

    priority = {
        512: 0,
        768: 1,
        640: 2,
        384: 3,
        1024: 4,
        1536: 5,
        256: 6,
    }
    priority_list = []
    for x in unique_lengths:
        priority_list.append((priority.get(x, 10), x, x))
    priority_list.sort()
    lengths = [x for _, _, x in priority_list[:7]]

    # Reserve time for polishing.
    reserve = max(180.0, 0.24 * total)
    init_deadline = max(start_t + 30.0, deadline - reserve)

    print("Initial exploration deadline: %.1fs" % (init_deadline - start_t), flush=True)

    # ---------------- Multi-length initial mirror search ----------------
    for n in lengths:
        if time.monotonic() >= init_deadline - 5.0:
            break

        if n == 512:
            plan_total = 430.0
        elif n in (640, 768):
            plan_total = 180.0
        elif n == 1024:
            plan_total = 150.0
        elif n == 384:
            plan_total = 95.0
        elif n == 1536:
            plan_total = 120.0
        else:
            plan_total = 80.0

        remaining = init_deadline - time.monotonic()
        plan_total = min(plan_total, max(25.0, remaining))
        if plan_total < 15.0:
            break

        items = generate_starts_tagged(n, rng, tracker.best)
        if not items:
            continue

        K = 4 if n <= 640 else 3
        chosen = screen_starts(items, n, K=K, must_tags=("parent", "half"))

        # One small parameterized global search on the best cheap initializer.
        if time.monotonic() < init_deadline - 20.0 and n <= 1536:
            base = None
            for a, tag in items:
                if tag == "half":
                    base = a
                    break
            if base is None:
                base = chosen[0][0] if chosen else half_binomial(n)
            param_deadline = min(
                time.monotonic() + min(70.0, 0.25 * plan_total),
                init_deadline - 15.0,
            )
            pa = run_parametric_search(base, param_deadline, D=5, rng=rng)
            tracker.update(pa, "param n=%d" % n)

        pending = []
        for a, tag in chosen:
            add_unique(pending, a)

        if tracker.best is not None:
            if tracker.best.size == n:
                add_unique(pending, tracker.best)
            else:
                add_unique(pending, resample_seq(tracker.best, n))

        if not pending:
            add_unique(pending, half_binomial(n))

        # Run mirror optimizer on the selected starts.
        rem = len(pending)
        per = plan_total / max(1, rem)
        for a in pending:
            rem -= 1
            if time.monotonic() >= init_deadline - 3.0:
                break
            allowed = min(per, max(5.0, (init_deadline - time.monotonic()) / max(1, rem + 1)))
            if allowed < 4.0:
                continue
            dlt = time.monotonic() + allowed
            best, J = run_mirror_optimize(
                a,
                deadline=dlt,
                rng=rng,
                gamma_start=5.0,
                gamma_end=180.0,
                mix_start=0.20,
                mix_end=0.05,
            )
            tracker.update(best, "mirror n=%d" % n)

    tracker.report("after initial exploration")

    # ---------------- Final polishing of the best sequence ----------------
    if tracker.best is not None:
        # Deep mirror polish with sharper peak focusing.
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
            dlt = min(time.monotonic() + 150.0, deadline - 30.0)
            best, J = run_mirror_optimize(
                tracker.best,
                deadline=dlt,
                rng=rng,
                gamma_start=30.0,
                gamma_end=500.0,
                mix_start=0.04,
                mix_end=0.0,
            )
            tracker.update(best, "mirror polish")

        # LP best-response polish.
        if tracker.best is not None and tracker.best.size <= 1024 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0:
            dlt = min(time.monotonic() + 110.0, deadline - 35.0)
            best = run_lp_mirror_descent(tracker.best, deadline=dlt, max_iters=90)
            tracker.update(best, "LP polish")

        # L-BFGS smooth-max polish.
        if tracker.best is not None and tracker.best.size <= 1536 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
            dlt = min(time.monotonic() + 80.0, deadline - 20.0)
            best = run_lbfgs_optimize(tracker.best, deadline=dlt, tau=100.0)
            tracker.update(best, "L-BFGS 100")

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0:
                dlt = min(time.monotonic() + 45.0, deadline - 10.0)
                best = run_lbfgs_optimize(tracker.best, deadline=dlt, tau=300.0)
                tracker.update(best, "L-BFGS 300")

        # Symmetric / reversed local refinements.
        if tracker.best is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 15.0:
            for label, base in (("ref_rev", tracker.best[::-1]),
                                 ("ref_sym", 0.5 * (tracker.best + tracker.best[::-1]))):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 8.0:
                    break
                dlt = min(time.monotonic() + 35.0, deadline - 8.0)
                best, J = run_mirror_optimize(
                    normalize_positive(base),
                    deadline=dlt,
                    rng=rng,
                    gamma_start=15.0,
                    gamma_end=350.0,
                    mix_start=0.03,
                    mix_end=0.0,
                )
                tracker.update(best, label)

        # Length extension attempt.
        if (
            tracker.best is not None
            and tracker.best.size < 2048
            and time.monotonic() < deadline - 10.0
        ):
            n_try = int(tracker.best.size * 2)
            if n_try > 2048:
                n_try = max(tracker.best.size + 256, 2048)
            if n_try > tracker.best.size and n_try <= 2048:
                a_ext = resample_seq(tracker.best, n_try)
                dlt = min(time.monotonic() + 55.0, deadline - 8.0)
                best, J = run_mirror_optimize(
                    a_ext,
                    deadline=dlt,
                    rng=rng,
                    gamma_start=8.0,
                    gamma_end=250.0,
                    mix_start=0.10,
                    mix_end=0.02,
                )
                tracker.update(best, "extend n=%d" % n_try)

    # Final cleanup and validation.
    final_seed = tracker.best if tracker.best is not None else half_binomial(512)
    final = cleanup_sequence(final_seed)
    tracker.update(final, "cleanup")

    seq = [float(x) for x in tracker.best if np.isfinite(x)]
    if len(seq) == 0:
        seq = [float(x) for x in half_binomial(512)]

    try:
        eval_score = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))
        if np.isfinite(eval_score) and eval_score < tracker.best_J:
            tracker.best_J = eval_score
            print("  evaluate_sequence adjusted score: %.9f" % eval_score, flush=True)
    except Exception:
        pass

    tracker.report("FINAL")
    return seq
```
